# topics: usage by place (2026-10-09)

Source: `reference/topic_usage.csv`, one row per topic (4205 in all), every place that stores its id as a column. Blank = 0. Same-name copies share a `group`.

## Same-name groups with two or more referenced copies (462 of 713 groups)

Groups where only one copy is referenced, or none, are in the CSV but skipped here.
